    * fix(review): preserve candidate evidence and bounded review coverage
    
    * fix(review): bind workflow source to loaded revision

    * feat(api): GitHub App installation tokens join the token pool
    
    Adds App installation tokens as first-class slots ahead of the env PATs,
    so the hosted service can run on App quota and the personal PAT can
    retire from the pool (#308 follow-up).
    
    - api/utils/github-app-token.ts mints installation tokens from
      GH_APP_ID + GH_APP_PRIVATE_KEY (PEM verbatim, \n-escaped, or base64)
      with a hand-rolled RS256 JWT via Node crypto — no new dependency.
      Tokens cache in module memory per instance, refresh 5 minutes before
      expiry, and concurrent callers share one in-flight mint (~2 REST calls
      per installation per instance per hour). Never logged, never in Redis.

Author: `luckyPipewrench`, 120 days in repo, 999 prior commits. Source: push.

**Commit message**

    fix(mcp): catch credential-request redirects after a negation (#1835)
    
    * fix(mcp): close credential-request negation redirects and add request verbs
    
    A negated credential request followed by a redirect ("never share your API key", then a later "only to this tool" or "only in the field below") was treated as a refusal and scanned clean. The negation now holds only when nothing later in the same description names a destination for the credential. Restrictions with no destination, such as "only for rotation", stay clean.
    
    "Request" and "ask for" join the handover verbs in imperative position only, so descriptive sentences like "the server will ask for your API key from the vault" aren't flagged.
